# SQL QUERY CATALOG — BPLUS ERP & POPSTAR LEGACY QUERIES

**Audit Date:** 2026-11-16 (Phase 3 Verified)  
**Parser Script:** [`popstar-business-platform-v4/tools/parse_report_csv.php`](../tools/parse_report_csv.php)  
**Sources Inspected:** `sql.csv` (1,145,303 bytes), `รายงาน.csv` (1,143,299 bytes), [`sync-agent/queries/bplus_*.sql`](../sync-agent/queries/), Legacy root PHP (`p_sale-BI.php` + 172 root files)  
**Execution Rule:** Non-destructive read-only static inspection ONLY (0 queries automatically executed against ERP).

---

## 1. Raw File Format Verification & Parser Results

| Property | `sql.csv` | `รายงาน.csv` |
|---|---|---|
| **File Size (bytes)** | 1,145,303 | 1,143,299 |
| **Encoding & BOM** | UTF-8 (`UTF-8-BOM (EF BB BF)`) | UTF-8 (`UTF-8-BOM (EF BB BF)`) |
| **Physical Lines** | 23,400 (`CRLF (23400)`) | 23,400 (`CRLF (23400)`) |
| **Delimiter** | `COMMA (,)` | `TAB (\t)` |
| **Field Quoting** | RFC-4180 double-quoted | unquoted (multiline SQL spans lines) (record starts with `^\d+\t\d+\t\d+\t`) |
| **Header Columns** | 32 (`RPF_KEY` .. `RPF_LASTUPD`) | 32 (`RPF_KEY` .. `RPF_LASTUPD`) |
| **Logical Report Rows** | **1,000** (`0` bad rows) | **1,000** (`0` bad rows) |

- **Cross-file Equivalence (`sql.csv` vs `รายงาน.csv`):** `1000` exact string matches, `0` whitespace diffs, `0` content diffs — **both files represent the exact same 1,000 Bplus `REPORTFILE` (`RPF_*`) rows.**
- **Parser Fix Applied (Phase 3):** Tightened `extractSqlTables()` in `tools/parse_report_csv.php` so `FROM (SELECT ...)` derived tables (e.g. `SQL11001205`) do not leak outer-`SELECT` column aliases (`DI_ACTIVE`, `ARCAT_CODE`) into the extracted table list.
- **Verification Command:**
```powershell
docker run --rm -v "d:\crm-member:/work:ro" popstar-platform-app:latest `
  php /work/popstar-business-platform-v4/tools/parse_report_csv.php /work/sql.csv "/work/รายงาน.csv"
```

## 2. Summary Statistics, Read-Only Audit & Parameter Bitmasks

### 2.1 High-Level Counts
- **Total Bplus Report Definitions:** `1,000` (`1,000` enabled `RPF_ENABLE = Y`)
- **Reports Containing SQL (`RPF_SQL` non-empty):** `404`
- **Crystal Reports Binary Only (`RPF_SQL` empty, `.rpt` logic only):** `596`
- **Read-Only Audit (`RPF_SQL`):** **`404 / 404` pure read-only `SELECT`**, **`0` INSERT / UPDATE / DELETE / EXEC / DDL / SELECT INTO** statements.
- **Workspace PHP Script Audit Contrast:** Among the `172` legacy PHP files in `d:\crm-member\`, `3` contain write/mutation keywords: `api_web_reserve.php` (MySQL `web_reserve_*` tables only), `p_sales_by_category_top_branch.php` (`INSERT INTO`), and `view_current_user.php` (`DELETE FROM` on `BPLUSCURRENTUSER`). Those legacy PHP scripts are marked `Unused` in V4 and excluded from ERP sync.

### 2.4 Document-Type Rules (`DOCTYPE.DT_PROPERTIES` & `DOCINFO.DI_ACTIVE`)
Proven from `SQL11001205`, `SQL11001103`, `SQL10501001`, `SQL11001043`, legacy [`p_sale-BI.php`](../../p_sale-BI.php), and V4 [`SalesRules.php`](../backend/app/Modules/ERPIntegration/SalesRules.php):

| `DT_PROPERTIES` | Bplus Semantics | Mentions in `sql.csv` | V4 `SalesRules.php` Handling | Real Sale Inclusion? |
|---|---|---:|---|---|
| **`302`** | Cash sale / tax invoice (ขายเงินสด) | 14 | `ACTUAL_SALE_PROPERTIES` (`doc_category = sale`) | **YES** (when `DI_ACTIVE = 0`) |
| **`307`** | Credit sale invoice (ขายเงินเชื่อ) | 13 | `ACTUAL_SALE_PROPERTIES` (`doc_category = sale`) | **YES** (when `DI_ACTIVE = 0`) |
| **`337`** | POS / alternate sale invoice (ขายสด/POS) | 12 | `ACTUAL_SALE_PROPERTIES` (`doc_category = sale`) | **YES** (when `DI_ACTIVE = 0`) |
| **`308`** | Return / Credit note (รับคืน / ลดหนี้) | 12 | `RETURN_PROPERTIES` (`doc_category = return`, stored negative) | **YES — SUBTRACT** (when `DI_ACTIVE = 0`) |
| **`207`** | Sales Reservation / Booking order (`AROE`, ใบจอง) | 4 | `RESERVATION_PROPERTIES` (`doc_category = reservation`) | **NO** (tracked separately; NOT completed sale) |
| `301`, `303..305`, `309..338` | Quotes, pro-forma, debit/misc AR/IC docs | 1–17 each | Excluded from `bplus_invoices.sql` | **NO** |
| `201..213` | Other order / reservation sub-types | 1–11 each | Excluded from `bplus_invoices.sql` | **NO** |
| `402..412`, `502..511`, `702..708` | AP purchases, IC stock transfers, Cheque/Bank | 1–5 each | Excluded (out of CRM/Member scope) | **NO** |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Observed Code Discrepancy Found in V4 `bplus_invoices.sql`:**
> - [`bplus_invoices.sql`](../sync-agent/queries/bplus_invoices.sql#L85-L133) fetches `DT_PROPERTIES IN (302, 307, 308, 337)` UNION `DT_PROPERTIES = 207`, **but lines 87 & 129 hardcode `DOCINFO.DI_DATE >= '20260101'` AND line 132 adds `WHERE doc_category <> 'reservation'`**, which suppresses `207` reservations AND omits `DI_ACTIVE` from `bplus_sales_items.sql` joins unless reconciled via `DOCINFO`. Meanwhile, existing PostgreSQL `erp_sales_invoices` already holds `9,247` rows from `2025-12-05 17:00+00` to `2025-12-31 17:00+00` (`5,001` Doc 307 posted + `4,130` Doc 207 reserved + `20` Doc 207 cancelled + `96` Doc 308 returned) before `sync_runs` id=186 failed on `Invoice 690101/100020001 has more than 200 lines; refusing incomplete sync.` (`local_bridge.ps1:450`). Aligning date window, `DI_ACTIVE = 0` status fidelity, >200 line-item pagination, and reservation rules is queued for **Phase 4 / Phase 6** reconciliation.
